# [CI] [AMD] ci: Register CUDA base/extra tests on AMD (parity experiment, do not merge)

[Upstream PR](https://github.com/sgl-project/sglang/pull/48317) · state: open · merged: False

## Original PR description

## Motivation

Experiment, **not for merge as is**. It answers one question: which of the tests that CUDA runs per PR (base) or per labeled PR (extra) also pass on ROCm, so that AMD CI can be aligned with NV by content.

## Modifications

- Registers 539 test files on AMD. Each one has an active CUDA base or extra registration and no AMD registration, and its name does not target a CUDA-only library or arch (`trtllm`, `fa3`/`fa4`, `flashinfer`, `flashmla`, `deepep`, `nvfp4`, `cutlass`, `deep_gemm`, `*_b200`, ...).
  - 300 of them mention no CUDA-only keyword.
  - 239 mention one in the body. Those usually pick a backend by arch and may need a ROCm branch.
- Each file maps by CUDA tier and GPU count:

  | CUDA | AMD suite | files |
  |---|---|---|
  | base 1-GPU (small / large) | `stage-b-test-1-gpu-{small,large}-amd` | 93 / 52 |
  | base 2-GPU | `stage-b-test-2-gpu-large-amd` | 15 |
  | base 4-GPU H100 | `stage-c-test-4-gpu-amd` | 1 |
  | base kernel unit / benchmark | `jit-kernel-{unit,benchmark}-test-amd` | 218 / 53 |
  | extra 1-GPU (small / large) | `extra-a-test-1-gpu-{small,large}-amd` | 13 / 20 |
  | extra 2-GPU | `extra-a-test-2-gpu-large-amd` | 17 |
  | extra 4-GPU | `extra-b-test-4-gpu-amd` | 34 |
  | extra 8-GPU | `extra-b-test-large-8-gpu-amd` (new job, MI300 8-GPU) | 23 |

- Left out:
  - 109 files whose names target a CUDA-only library or arch.
  - 56 B200/GB300-only base registrations.
  - The 13-file diffusion lane, which AMD runs through `multimodal_gen/test/run_suite.py`.
  - 2 files that already have AMD copies.
- `est_time` is 1.5x the CUDA live estimate.
- Shards are raised so every shard stays under its step timeout on those estimates: jit-kernel-unit 1 -> 10, benchmark 1 -> 2, stage-b 1-gpu-large 2 -> 4, 2-gpu-large 1 -> 2, extra-a 2/3/2 -> 3/6/4, and extra-b 4-GPU 1 -> 6.
- For this experiment only, extra-b 4-GPU runs on the 8-GPU MI300 pool, because the 4-GPU pool runs at most 2 jobs at once.

## Plan

Dispatch `pr-test-amd.yml` (the stages above) and `pr-test-amd-extra.yml` with `continue_on_error`, collect pass / fail / timeout per file, and split the passing files into reviewable PRs. Failing files get a ROCm fix or a `disabled=` reason. Results are posted below.

## Interim results (base run: one stage-b shard and stage-c 4-GPU still pending; extra run complete)

Findings so far:
- **All 261 already-registered AMD tests in these jobs passed** except `basic_perf/test_score_api.py` (extra), so the new failures are the new tests, not the environment.
- 282 of the 539 new files pass on MI300 as is. 65 skip every case on ROCm (exit code 5: CUDA-only guards), so they only need the registration dropped or a ROCm path.
- JIT kernel failures are mostly kernels with no ROCm build: JIT modules that fail to compile under HIP (`moe_topk_sigmoid`, `moe_route`, `causal_conv1d`, `rotary_embedding`, `fused_gemma_qknorm_rope`), fp8 tests that pass `float8_e4m3fn` where MI300 needs fnuz, missing `sgl_kernel` ops (`rmsnorm`), and CUDA-only kwargs (`launch_pdl`).
- 2 timeouts and 6 not-run files are in the 4/8-GPU extra shards (est_time too low).

Results so far (per file, from `run_suite.py`'s TIMINGS block). `skipped-all` = exit code 5, i.e. every test case in the file skipped itself (CUDA-only guards); `not-run` = the shard ended before reaching the file (step timeout) or is still running.

| AMD suite | pass | fail | timeout | skipped-all | not-run |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| `extra-a-test-1-gpu-large-amd` | 4 | 2 | 0 | 14 | 0 |
| `extra-a-test-1-gpu-small-amd` | 8 | 3 | 0 | 2 | 0 |
| `extra-a-test-2-gpu-large-amd` | 5 | 5 | 0 | 7 | 0 |
| `extra-b-test-4-gpu-amd` | 9 | 6 | 1 | 18 | 0 |
| `extra-b-test-large-8-gpu-amd` | 4 | 4 | 1 | 8 | 6 |
| `jit-kernel-benchmark-test-amd` | 19 | 34 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| `jit-kernel-unit-test-amd` | 141 | 77 | 0 | 0 | 0 |
| `stage-b-test-1-gpu-large-amd` | 21 | 22 | 0 | 9 | 0 |
| `stage-b-test-1-gpu-small-amd` | 61 | 15 | 0 | 5 | 12 |
| `stage-b-test-2-gpu-large-amd` | 10 | 3 | 0 | 2 | 0 |
| **total** | **282** | **171** | **2** | **65** | **18** |
